Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on mass_spec structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 373. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 392 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the mass_spec space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 373 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 392. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the mass_spec structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 629. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 114 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 629 + O(1/n^114). Summing over all modes and applying the mass_spec structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
